日本：7月以降の設備投資需要はAI関連産業を中心に旺盛
3
 7～9月期の機械受注は増勢回復へ。AI関連業種からの受注がけん引
― ①旺盛なAI需要のもと、関連業種では設備不足感が強まっていたこと、②輸出増が持続するもとで、好業績が期待され
ること、③中東情勢の不確実性が後退したことなどが背景
 先行き設備投資は緩やかな増勢を辿る見込み
― 半導体製造装置をはじめ、AI・データセンター関連品目を中心に受注残が増加しており、目先こうした傾向が続く見通し
― 積み上がった受注残を段階的に消化することで、設備投資が持続的に下支えされる公算が大きい

世界：AI関連投資の拡大が鉱物需要を押し上げる一方、鈍い資源開発投資の回復ペース
4
 AI関連需要の拡大が銅やレアアースの需給環境に影響
― データセンター建設や電力インフラ整備に伴い、銅やレアアースの需要が拡大。ガリウム等の戦略鉱物への需要も増加
 資源開発投資の回復が遅れるなか、中長期的な供給不足への懸念強まる
―IEAは、2035年に銅が供給量対比で約26％の供給不足に陥ると予測。レアアースは供給不足を回避する見込みながら、

採掘・精錬において中国が支配的地位を占めており、供給元の集中がリスク
IEA：銅、レアアースの需要見通し IEA：銅・レアアースの需給バランス予測（2035年）
（出所）IEA "Global Critical Minerals Outlook 2026"より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
AI関連投資の拡大を背景に、
銅やレアアースの需要が拡大
銅は供給が不足へ
レアアースは供給超過も供給元の集中がリスク
注目点①AI投資拡大と資源供給制約

世界：AI投資が金融市場を下支えする一方、持続性への懸念は残存
 IMFはAI投資や企業収益の拡大を背景に金融市場が底堅さを維持していると評価
― ただし、金融安定リスクは引き続き高いとの評価で、AI、国債市場、サイバーを主要なリスク要因として指摘
 AIについてはAI主導の市場調整リスクを警戒
―AI中核企業の収益拡大によって株価の割高感は緩和する一方、社債発行や循環的な資金調達が拡大。投資収益が期
待を下回った場合、金融市場への影響が増幅される可能性
5

世界：国債市場に残る脆弱性。インフレ・財政懸念による金利急騰リスクは増大
 主要国の長期金利は、期待短期金利とタームプレミアムの双方から上昇
―IMFは財政懸念などを背景にタームプレミアムが歴史的に高い領域にあると指摘
― 国債発行の増加や借換リスクの高まりに加え、価格感応度の高い投資家の存在がインフレ・財政ショックによる金利上
昇を増幅する可能性
― 国債市場は足元で秩序を維持するも、急激な金利上昇は資金調達市場の逼迫や銀行部門への波及を招くリスク

### Page 8

世界：ヘッジファンドのレバレッジ拡大が市場ショックを増幅、銀行部門への波及にも警戒
 高まるヘッジファンドの存在感と市場への影響力
― ヘッジファンドの総資産は2013年の約4兆ドルから2026年初には約13兆ドルに拡大。平時には市場の流動性や効率性
を支える一方、ストレス時にはレバレッジ解消や取引集中が価格変動を増幅させる懸念
 ヘッジファンドの損失がプライムブローカーを通じて銀行部門に波及するリスク
―IMFはヘッジファンドの運用成績悪化がプライムブローカーの収益にも悪影響を及ぼすと分析。銀行自身の健全性に加
え、ノンバンクとの相互連関を通じた金融システム全体への波及にも警鐘
7

世界：2026年の世界経済は底堅く推移も、経済成長の恩恵やインフレの負担は一部に偏在
 IMFは2026年の世界成長率を小幅に上方修正（3.0％→3.1％）
―AIブームを追い風とした半導体輸出の増加等を背景に、アジアを中心に上方修正
― 備蓄放出・代替調達等による政府・企業の対応が原油供給ショックによる下押しを緩和したことも影響
 一方、AI需要の取り込みや供給ショックへの耐性には国家間で格差
―IMFによれば、世界の経済成長の95％を占める国の数は54（4月）から32（10月）に減少したほか、今次改定では47％
の国が成長率を下方修正。AIブームの恩恵や供給ショックによる負担は一部の国・地域に偏在

11

 フィリピンでAZEC（閣僚会合）とAEBFが同時期に開催。ASEANのエネルギー安全保障に関心が高まる

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み
（注） コメは玄米及び精米。食品関連は、飲食料品及び農林水産物（除くコメ）

（出所） 日本銀行「企業物価指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
国内企業物価 輸入物価（円建て）と国内企業物価
（注） 輸入物価は1カ月先行。原油輸入単価とドバイ原油（1カ月先行）を使用し、輸入物価（契
約通貨建て）の先行きを試算し、為替は1ドル157円程度の推移を想定。二重線部分は見
通し
（出所） 日本銀行「企業物価指数」、財務省「貿易統計」、IMF、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所

## Document 030: 20261015【投研報告】市場快訊_通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261015【投研報告】市場快訊_通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (市場評論)
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期 ，Fed 再升息風險重新升高 ：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期 ，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本 ：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」 ，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」 。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出 ，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的 「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」 ，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展 ， 引發市場對未來DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮 ， 記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵 ， 仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期 。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺 ， 留意10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

### Page 4

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡 ：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。
資料來源：S&P Global PMI, Bureau of Economic Analysis via S&P Global Market
Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數
• 目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ：最新民調顯示，民主黨
於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響
資料來源：美國銀行，2026.7
資料來源：復華投信，2026.9

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網
• 上兆美元的流動性護城河： 儘管債務增加，但科技十二巨頭合計持有近 1兆美元的等值現金，整體科技板塊流動性充裕 ，短
期內無流動性危機。

能力顯著降低槓桿比率的上升壓力，確保信評機構維護穩定的信用評級。
• 資本配置策略分化：(1)兼顧股東回報：蘋果、微軟、Meta與Alphabet在投入AI同時，仍維持大規模的庫藏股買回與股息發
放。(2)全數投入資本支出：亞馬遜將絕大部分現金導向資本支出(2026年資本支出預計達2,200億美元居首)，且不發放股息。
• 小結與投資建議：科技巨頭為維持AI技術與基礎設施領先優勢，短期內，負自由現金流將成為常態。然而，這並非財務危機

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管

濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。

Industry View In-Line
At the rack level, Maia 300 is the key incremental story, while
Nvidia/AMD exhibits offered limited new system-level hardware.
Nvidia’s MGX component upgrades are the main supply-chain
watch points, although PTFE and next-gen interconnect and
cooling connector (ZQD) adoption remains uncertain.

Nvidia/AMD –  focus on ramps rather than new platforms. Most ODM displays
were similar to Computex Taipei (early June), so timing of ramp is key. For Nvidia,
VR200 racks have started a small-volume ramp, with the Nvidia Groq 3 LPX rack
following in late 2026/early 2027 and standalone Vera CPU racks in late 1Q27/early
2Q27, based on our checks. For AMD, Helios remained largely unchanged with
recent checks, with racks ramping in 1Q27, with pilot-build observations pointing to

Shennan Circuits for EAM PCBs and Fositek/Parker for quick disconnects.
Maia 300 -  a larger in-house inference opportunity. Microsoft plans production
deployments in 2027 as it scales Maia toward multi-gigawatt deployments. Each
rack integrates 96 accelerators within 167 kW. Based on that, each gigawatt of
deployment would imply 5 to 5.5K racks, and we believe Hon Hai and Wiwynn could
be the ODM partners, while our pilot-build observations point to ISU Petasys for the

accelerator PCB, AVC for fans, and Fositek/Staubli for quick disconnects.
PTFE - technical upside, but no confirmed timing of adoption. Nvidia is evaluating
PTFE for Rubin Ultra on Oberon following the Kyber delay and showcased its
NVLink PTFE PCB with seven suppliers (though we think there are suppliers missing
from this list). Better signal performance and reduced reliance on Q glass are
potential benefits, but processing complexity, higher costs and yield/qualification

risks remain hurdles to commercial adoption.
Ultra Dense Interconnect - next-generation interconnect opportunity for FIT.
Nvidia showcased six suppliers for this next-generation interconnect, which is
intended to replace Amphenol’s current HD2 connector in midplane and cable
cartridge applications. However, we believe adoption is still about two years away. In
the meantime, the supply chain is working to improve yields and bring ASPs below
US$100, with pogo-pin supply currently a bottleneck. Our checks identify FIT as the

key design partner for Kyber’s planned board-to-board connector, a position that
bodes well for its supply share once the product ramps.
ZQD - higher flow density and an expected ASP premium. Nvidia’s new ZQD
targets higher flow rates per unit area and was displayed with 14 suppliers.
Although the supply chain is working to improve yields and lower costs, greater
design complexity should keep ASPs above current QD pricing of US$20–30.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Major Takeaways from OCP Summit 2026
ODM/OEM: Almost all the ODM/OEMs had a booth at OCP, but during our visit, we feel
most of the products were largely similar to what was showcased at Computex, such as
the Vera Rubin NVL72 rack, the LPX tray, the Vera standalone tray. So net, we think from a
hardware perspective, there was nothing new on Nvidia or AMD. We believe the Nvidia
VR200 rack is already starting ramp in small volume, with the LPX rack starting to ramp

later this year or early next year, and the Vera standalone CPU rack ramping late 1Q27 or
early 2Q27.
AMD: Wiwynn, Meta, and AMD each showcased the Helios rack, which was unchanged
from last year’s OCP display with one exception: this year’s version featured the actual
compute tray in place of last year’s dummy tray, allowing a closer look. That said, the
compute tray itself was not new to us, as we had already seen it at OCP Taiwan in August.
• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe Shennan Circuits is one

of the pilot build PCB suppliers for the MI455 Helios EAM PCB.
• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe Fositek (MQD02) and
Parker (UQD04) are one of the pilot build quick disconnect connector suppliers for
the MI455 Helios compute tray.
• HPE displayed the Helios switch tray at the OCP Summit. We believe the other
supplier of the Helios switch trays is Celestica.
Microsoft: Maia 300 represents a major expansion of Microsoft’s in-house inference

infrastructure. Announced on October 12, 2026, with production deployments planned for
2027, it forms part of Microsoft’s commitment to scale the Maia family to multi-gigawatt
deployments and make it the primary inference platform for its products and services. The
design targets long-context reasoning, conversational AI, and multi-step agentic
workloads, with an emphasis on improving inference economics through coordinated
silicon, software, and system design.

Maia 300 rack integrates 96 accelerators within a 167 kW power envelope, providing
approximately 27.6 TB of aggregate HBM capacity. There are 4 accelerators per tray,
implying 24 compute trays per rack, including six high-radix switch ASICs that are
integrated into three liquid-cooled switch trays, providing the rack-local connectivity
required by all 96 accelerators. Microsoft projects 40% more tokens per megawatt than
leading GPU-based systems and up to 7× Maia 200’s rack-level token throughput, though

this takes into account that the Maia 300 rack  includes twice as many accelerators per
rack.
• The Official Microsoft Blog A two-tier Ethernet scale-up network connects up to
128 racks, or 12,288 accelerators, while liquid cooling removes more than 80% of
rack heat. Each compute tray includes 8x fans  (Exhibit 3 ), and based on our
observations at the OCP Summit, we believe AVC is one of the pilot build fan
suppliers for the sample compute tray.

• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe ISU Petasys is one of
the pilot build PCB suppliers for Microsoft’s Maia 300 AI accelerator.
• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe Fositek and Staubli are
pilot build MQD03 (quick disconnect connector) suppliers for the compute tray.

Nvidia MGX Ecosystem
PTFE: We previously wrote that our supply chain checks suggest that Nvidia had initially
planned to adopt PTFE CCL for the very high-layer-count midplane in its Kyber
architecture. With Kyber now delayed, and Rubin Ultra no longer expected to use that
architecture, as previously discussed, the focus has shifted back to the Oberon
architecture. Nvidia is again evaluating PTFE for Rubin Ultra on Oberon, and at this year’s

OCP Summit, Nvidia showcased its NVLink PTFE PCB, with a total of 7 suppliers, including
VGT, TTM, Taiflex, SYTECH, Shennan Circuits, Kinwong, and Flexium (Exhibit 4  & Exhibit
5 ). However, our checks indicate that actual adoption for Rubin Ultra remains uncertain.
However, we find the supplier list puzzling. Our checks suggest that WUS PCB and
Unimicron are also working on this solution, and that Flexium, which has collaborated
with other PCB suppliers, may handle only a few process steps. Our checks also indicate

that Shennan Circuits, WUS PCB and VGT are now the leading PCB makers for this
solution, with relatively higher yields.
Advantages of PTFE
• Best signal performance: the lowest Dk and Df of the mainstream options, below
even standard M9-class CCLs. This matters most where insertion-loss budgets are
tightest, i.e. the highest speeds and highest layer counts.
• Stable electricals: the low Dk/Df hold up across frequency and temperature, and
PTFE absorbs very little moisture, so performance is stable in use.

• Less dependence on Q glass: because PTFE’s intrinsic dielectric constant is already
very low, reaching the target Dk requires less (and in some hybrid constructions,
none) of the expensive, supply-constrained Q glass. That is both a cost lever and a
supply-security benefit.
Disadvantages of PTFE

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
• Mechanically soft: low modulus makes it difficult to handle and to hold to
tolerance.
• Difficult lamination: PTFE does not flow like a thermoset, so bonding needs either
very high temperatures or lower-melting fluoropolymer (such as PFA
(polyfluoroalkoxy))/thermoset bond layers; its high, non-linear CTE stresses
copper adhesion and complicates layer-to-layer registration, and pressing typically
requires vacuum or an inert atmosphere with longer cycles.

• Difficult drilling: the soft, gummy resin tends to smear and can tear, and any
ceramic filler is abrasive on tooling. Also, the hole wall will not accept electroless
copper without an added activation step (sodium etch or plasma) on top of normal
desmear.
• Higher cost and qualification risk: materials and fabrication are both more
expensive, the qualified supply base is narrower, and yields at very high layer
counts carry risk.
Exhibit 4: Nvidia's NVLink PTFE Board

Source: Nvidia, Morgan Stanley Research.

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
Exhibit 5: Nvidia PTFE PCB Supplier List
Source: Nvidia, Morgan Stanley Research.
Ultra Dense Interconnect: Currently, Nvidia's NVL72 racks are using Amphenol's Paladin
HD2 connectors for VR200 NVL72 midplanes and cable cartridges. Our previous checks
done around Computex 2026 in June, indicate ASP could reach US$70-90+ per set. For
the next-generation Kyber racks, our checks have previously indicated that a new midplane
connector called "Voronoi" will be introduced, and FIT is the key design partner for this

new board-to-board connector. At this year’s OCP Summit, Nvidia showcased a new
connector it calls the “Ultra Dense Interconnect”, with six suppliers in total. This was our
first in-person look at the design. We believe adoption is still two years out, and the
supply chain is currently focused on improving yields and optimizing costs to bring the
ASP below US$100. Our checks suggest that pogo pin supply is currently a bottleneck for
this connector.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Exhibit 6: Nvidia next-generation Ultra Dense Interconnect
Source: Nvidia, Morgan Stanley Research.
ZQD: At COMPUTEX in Taipei earlier this year, we noted that our checks indicated Nvidia
was planning to introduce a new quick disconnect (QD) design for next-generation server
racks to achieve higher flow rates per unit area as cooling requirements rise. At this year’s
OCP Summit, Nvidia showcased that design, called ZQD, with 14 suppliers in total. Our

checks indicate the supply chain is also working to improve yields and optimize costs to
bring ZQD ASPs down. However, given the greater complexity of the design, we expect
ZQD ASPs to remain above current QD pricing of US$20–30 per set.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

嘉澤：全球前三大 CPU Socket 供應商，伺服器已成營收主軸
全球前三大中央處理器插座CPU socket供應商。依Bishop統計在電腦暨週邊連
接器廠商中全球排名第四，不分行業別的全球連接器排名全球排名第十八。
主要客戶為AMD與Intel。產品涵蓋 CPU Socket、記憶體插槽、高速連接器及
線材，並延伸至 SOCAMM、液冷快接頭與 NPO Socket。
本研究預估，嘉澤伺服器營收由 2026 年221.14 億元增至 2027 年 328.67 億元，
年增 48.6%。成長來自板量回升、CPU新平台提高 Socket 與高速連接器價值，
以及 AI 新產品放量；PC 相關營收占比則由 2025 年 36.60%，降至 2027 年
16.5%，營運重心持續轉向伺服器。

CPU socket是甚麼
CPU 插槽的設計目的是將 CPU 處理器牢固地固定在主機板上，並在設備移動時
有助於防止潛在的損壞。嘉澤主要客戶為AMD與Intel。
資料來源 :premioinc.com
PGA (插針網格陣列)BGA (球柵陣列)LGA (平面網格陣列)項目
針腳在CPU上，插入主
機板的孔位中。
CPU 底部是微小錫球，直
接高溫焊接在主機板上。
CPU 底部是平貼金屬觸點，
針腳做在主機板插槽上。外觀結構
可以。直接拉起拉桿即
可拔插更換。
不行。屬於永久焊接，需

卸。
可以。可自由解鎖扣具、更

1. 針腳在 CPU 上，輕
微彎曲時通常還能手動
扳正修復。
1. 體積最薄、最省空間。
適合行動裝置。

散熱表現優異。
2.高密度高上限：相同面積
內可容納更多觸點，能支援
更多通道與更高供電，技術
天花板最高。

理體積大，無法做到高
密度排列，高頻與多通
道（如 DDR5）訊號完
整性不如 LGA。

永久焊接，壞任何一個晶

板。

板插槽針腳如毛髮般細小，
清理或安裝時極易壓歪，且
幾乎無法手動修復。

代理 AI 讓 CPU 工作量增加：從回覆問題，走向執行任務
原先：用戶提出問題；應用程式向模型發送提示；模型生成答案；應用程式將答
案傳回給用戶。主節點CPU負責調度、I/O和系統管理，而GPU則負責繁重的運
算任務。
現在：代理AI不再只是回答一個問題，而是將目標分解成多個步驟，決定下一步
該做什麼，調用多個模型，查詢資料庫，連接API，運行企業應用程序，檢查權
限，檢索內存，驗證輸出，然後再次循環執行整個過程。
CPU負責：編排：管理分解複雜任務的引擎。代理執行和工具呼叫：觸發 API 和
傳統企業軟體。策略與安全：對每個自主操作進行實際檢查。
隨 AI 從模型推論延伸至 Agent 執行，CPU 的角色擴及任務協調、工具操作及資
料處理。AMD 5 月官方文章已描述 CPU：GPU 由過去約 1：4–8，朝 1：1 移
動；Intel Q2 法說進一步表示，目前以顆數計已接近均衡。兩家原廠的觀察，支
持 CPU 在 AI 基礎建設中重要性提高

資料來源 :AMD官網(智能體人工智慧改變了CPU/GPU的平衡)
5

AI 基礎設施正在從「以 GPU 為中心」的架構，走向「GPU 與 CPU 分工協
作」的分散式架構。未來不是只增加 GPU，而是新增獨立的 Agentic CPU
compute layer
New Agentic CPU Racks：新增的 Agent 執行層負責 Agent Control
Plane 與 Tool Execution管理 agent 的任務拆解、工作流調度、狀態管理與
多 agent 協作執行 API 呼叫、SQL 查詢、文件檢索、資料處理、程式執行與
外部工具調用處理權限控管、稽核紀錄、 安全檢查、重試與人工審批核心功
能是把 LLM 的「決策」轉換成企業系統中的「實際行動」

Meta Muse：個人代理產品化，使 CPU 執行環境的需求更具體
Muse 的投資意義在於：大型消費平台開始提供可持續執行任務的個人代理，使
CPU 沙盒需求從技術架構走向實際服務。
與AMD的關係Meta已部署數百萬顆AMD EPYC，並是Venice、Verano 的領先
客戶。因此，Meta 的運算擴張是 AMD CPU 需求的重要觀察對象。
持續在線的虛擬機（Persistent VMs）： Meta Muse 的底層採用了 Muse
Secure VM 架構。這些 VM 即使在使用者沒有主動點開 App 時，也在後台持續
運作，消耗大量的作業系統配置、記憶體管理與安全驗證資源—這些全部屬於通
用運算，只有 CPU 能做。因此當億萬名用戶的 AI 代理同時在線，CPU 的短缺
會遠比 GPU 來得更強烈。Muse 這樣的個人代理規模不斷擴大，對 CPU 的需求
勢必會增長，預計 CPU 與 GPU 的比例將從 1:1往4:1以上走 (intel電話會議)

資料來源 :trendforce, meta
對運算需求的意義Muse 是甚麼
大型平台投入代理服務產品
化
2026 年 9 月 8 日推出個人
AI 代理
執行環境需要 CPU、記憶體
及系統資源
使用專屬安全虛擬機 Muse
Secure VM
工具操作與程式執行增加具備瀏覽器，可跨應用處理工作
工作量不完全取決於即時聊
天次數

Meta muse帶動AI agent趨勢，使CPU類股上漲
 Meta Muse 在 9 月 8 日剛上線時，市場仍在觀望它與傳統的問答機器人（如 ChatGPT）
有何不同。直到 9 月 18 日（上線第 10 天），第三方研調機構 Sensor Tower 釋出數據，
顯示 Muse 已經連續數天登頂美國 App Store 免費榜冠軍，13 天內累計下載突破 250 萬
次。這時市場才驚覺，這是一個「現象級爆款」。
 Muse 可以實際幫用戶串接 Gmail、自動比價，甚至與 Shopify 的 Shop Pay 整合代客結
帳。當週一（9 月 21 日）開盤，市場資金首先推升 Meta 股價大漲 11%。CPU類股全面
大漲10%，嘉澤隔日也開盤即漲停，顯示市場有定位上的連動。(如下圖)

開發NPO socket，為未來潛在商機

NPO的核心定義是：將光引擎從傳統的前面板可插拔位置，移動到更靠近交
換晶片（ASIC） 的地方，但光引擎和ASIC仍然是兩個獨立的封裝體。它們通
常被並排安裝在同一塊高性能基板（如矽中介層或有機基板）上
NPO Socket已進入客戶最後認證階段，且達可量產狀態，量產目標落在2027
年第3季，目前未納入估計，等待未來公司給更多指引。
AI資料中心在Scale-up與Scale-out兩種架構同步拉高頻寬需求，嘉澤在NPO、
既有高速銅傳輸與高功率連接方案的產品組合，將對應不同平台導入時程。

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

### Page 1

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다. 
Buy (Maintain)

## Document 233: AI的饼越画越大，特斯拉为何仍难打动华尔街？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI的饼越画越大，特斯拉为何仍难打动华尔街？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

三⼤投⾏罕⻅地形成共识：进展真实存在，但估值已充分定价，甚⾄过于饱满。特斯拉正
⾯临⼀个愈发棘⼿的困境：故事越讲越⼤，估值却越来越难以⽀撑。
摘要：
特斯拉第三季度财报显⽰，公司⾃动驾驶、储能和机器⼈业务均取得阶段性进展，⽑利率亦超
出预期，但居⾼不下的运营⽀出、低于预期的盈利能⼒以及依然遥远的商业化兑现时间表，令
华尔街主流投⾏维持观望⽴场。这家市值 1.6 万亿美元的公司，正⾯临⼀个愈发棘⼿的困境：故
事越讲越⼤，估值却越来越难以⽀撑。
特斯拉第三季度营收 280 亿美元， EBIT 仅 7.18 亿美元，远低于⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利的预期，运营⽀
出超预期是主要拖累。尽管⾮ GAAP 每股摊薄收益 0.48 美元⼩幅超预期，但⾼盛已将 2026 ⾄
2027 年 EPS 预测下调，理由正是持续攀升的运营成本。对于股价⽽⾔，⾼盛维持中性评级及 360
美元⽬标价，较当前股价隐含约 7% 的下⾏空间；摩根⼠丹利同样维持中性评级，⽬标价 400 美
元；摩根⼤通对特斯拉股票标注中性评级。
三⼤投⾏罕⻅地形成共识：进展真实存在，但估值已充分定价，甚⾄过于饱满。

尽管如此，财报中的亮点同样不容忽视。北美 FSD 新⻋搭载率从⼆季度的 55% 升⾄超 75% ，⽆
⼈驾驶 Robotaxi 累计⾥程在短短数周内从 100 万英⾥增⾄ 310 万英⾥；储能业务⽑利率升⾄
33.1% ， Megapack 积压订单创历史纪录。这些数据为特斯拉的⻓期叙事提供了真实⽀撑，也是
多数分析师尚未彻底放弃这只股票的底层逻辑。
⽑利率惊喜，利润叙事受阻
特斯拉三季度整体⽑利率 19.6% ，全⾯超出各⽅预期 —— ⾼盛预测 18.5% ，公司汇总市场⼀致预
期 17.9% ， FactSet ⼝径为 17.5% 。
其中，汽⻋业务⽑利率（含股权激励，剔除碳积分）达 18.1% ，超出⾼盛预测的 16.3% ；储能业
务⽑利率 33.1% ，亦⼤幅超出摩根⼠丹利预测的 26.5% ；服务业务⽑利率 15.9% ，创历史新⾼。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

然⽽，⽑利率层⾯的超预期被费⽤端的失控所抵消。三季度 EBIT 仅 7.18 亿美元，明显低于⾼盛
预测的 10.43 亿美元和摩根⼠丹利预测的 5.26 亿美元；研发费⽤ 27.61 亿美元，较摩根⼠丹利预
测⾼出约 11% 。管理层明确表⽰，为⽀撑 AI 与能源业务扩张 —— 包括 Optimus 、 Robotaxi 及
Megapack—— 运营⽀出和资本开⽀将继续按季环⽐增⻓。
⾼盛随即下调 2026 ⾄ 2027 年 EPS 预测，分别从 0.82 美元和 1.75 美元下修⾄ 0.67 美元和 1.60 美
元，同时略微上调 2028 年预测⾄ 2.50 美元。
摩根⼤通亦对此持审慎态度。按⽬前股价计算， 2028 年预期市盈率仍⾼达 154 倍，⾃由现⾦流收
益率在 2026 ⾄ 2027 年均为负值，这意味着投资者需要为遥远的未来⽀付极⾼的溢价。
Robotaxi 与 FSD ：⾥程碑在加速，但商业化仍在早期
Robotaxi 是本季度最受市场关注的业务进展。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 18 ⽇，特斯拉完全⽆⼈驾驶的 Robotaxi

累计⾏驶⾥程达 310 万英⾥，较 9 ⽉ 3 ⽇的约 100 万英⾥增⻓超过两倍，周增速约 20% ，超出⾼盛
此前预期的 200 ⾄ 300 万英⾥区间上限。
据⾼盛研究报告， 2026 年 1 ⽉ 22 ⽇特斯拉开始完全⽆监督运营⾄今，平均每约 10 万英⾥发⽣⼀
次事故（⽆论责任归属或严重程度）；相⽐之下， Waymo 在 2025 年 7 ⽉⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉间平均每 18
万英⾥发⽣⼀次，近期数据已达每 36 万英⾥⼀次。差距依然存在，但特斯拉改善速度不可忽
视。
在地理扩张层⾯，管理层表⽰ Cybercab 已在奥斯汀展开运营，并预计下⽉在达拉斯启动；圣安
东尼奥和杰克逊维尔被列⼊后续扩张城市名单，此前已公布的凤凰城和拉斯维加斯亦在推进
中。
FSD 主动订阅⽤⼾数达 174 万，同⽐增⻓ 67% ；欧盟范围内的全⾯审批最早预计在 2026 年 12
⽉，管理层⽬标是在 2027 年⼀季度前获得欧盟统⼀许可。尽管进展积极，但⾼盛认为来⾃
Waymo 等竞争对⼿的压⼒不容低估，并将⾃动驾驶领域的竞争列为主要下⾏⻛险之⼀。

Optimus ：节奏⽐市场预期保守
⼈形机器⼈ Optimus 是本季度另⼀个被⾼度审视的议题。
⻢斯克在财报电话会上确认 Optimus 已在弗⾥蒙特⼯⼚开始量产，但对产能爬坡的表述相当审慎
——12 个⽉内⽬标产量为 1 万⾄ 2 万台，低于部分市场此前的乐观预期，这批机器⼈将⾸先⽤于
特斯拉内部的 Tesla Academy 进⾏⾃我训练和数据⽣成。⾯向外部客⼾的商业销售⽬标被推⾄
2027 年底，消费者端应⽤则更为遥远。
⻢斯克将 Optimus 的量产过程描述为 "S 曲线 " ，强调需要 " ⼀万件事同时运转正确 " ，速度最慢的零
部件或⼯艺将决定整体爬坡节奏。他点名⼿部（灵巧⼿）是当前最⼤的硬件瓶颈，实现⼈类级
别的灵活性和⻓时间可靠运作仍是挑战。
算⼒层⾯，特斯拉计划于 2027 年 4 ⽉将新⼀代 AI5 芯⽚集成进 Optimus ，该芯⽚算⼒据管理层介
绍约为现有 AI4 芯⽚的 10 倍，将⼤幅提升复杂⾏为能⼒。
据摩根⼤通研究报告，其对整个⼈形机器⼈供应链的⽴场因此趋于谨慎，并对 Unitree 、 Estun 、

UBTech 在未来 1 ⾄ 3 个⽉内⾯临预期修正⻛险保持警惕，。
储能与汽⻋需求：实质性亮点

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
在⼏项容易被 AI 叙事遮盖的核⼼业务中，储能和汽⻋需求实际上提供了令⼈信服的基本⾯⽀撑。
储能业务⽑利率 33.1% ，环⽐⼤幅提升；管理层称 Megapack 容量 "2027 年⼏乎已售罄 " ，能源业
务积压订单创历史纪录。德克萨斯州 Megafactory 已开始⽣产。
值得注意的是，三季度储能营收 28.81 亿美元低于⾼盛预测的 37.49 亿美元，管理层将部分差距
归因于对能源客⼾的关税退款；⾼盛表⽰将继续跟踪 ASP （平均售价）⾛势及定价环境。
汽⻋需求⽅⾯，管理层称乘⽤⻋积压订单处于四年最⾼⽔平，已在加拿⼤、澳⼤利亚、⽇本和
中国台湾实现创纪录交付， Tesla Semi 也开始量产。⾼盛将 2026 ⾄ 2028 年交付量预测上调⾄

184 万 /195 万 /203 万辆。供应链层⾯，电池可⽤性仍是全球产能爬坡的限制因素之⼀，但公司正
在柏林、德克萨斯和内华达多地推进相关产能建设。
估值 " 已充分定价 "
尽管业务进展不乏亮点，华尔街主流机构对特斯拉股票的态度依然⾼度⼀致。
⾼盛维持中性评级， 12 个⽉⽬标价 360 美元，基于 150 倍市盈率乘以未来 5 ⾄ 8 个季度 EPS 预测
（含股权激励），较当前股价约有 7% 的下⾏空间。摩根⼠丹利维持中性评级，⽬标价 400 美元，
其估值模型包含 44 美元的核⼼汽⻋业务、 145 美元的⽹络服务、 120 美元的 Tesla Mobility 、 35 美
元的储能，以及折扣后 56 美元的⼈形机器⼈价值分配，已相当充分地反映了⻓期期权价值。
⾼盛明确指出，当前估值 " 已充分定价 " （ valuation is full ），盈利能⼒和⾃由现⾦流在近⾄中期将
持续承压，⽽ EPS 改善的幅度和节奏将受到多个领域竞争压⼒的约束。特斯拉的 12 个⽉股价与

标普 500 指数相⽐，过去⼀年已跑输约 24% ，反映出市场在等待兑现的过程中持续消化溢价。
此外，⾼盛表⽰，特斯拉 CEO 在财报电话会上提及， SpaceX 于 10 ⽉ 20 ⽇的财报电话会上披露，
SpaceX 与特斯拉已分别成⽴由独⽴董事组成的特别委员会，就潜在业务合并进⾏讨论。这⼀披
露为特斯拉股票增添了新的变量，但当前信息有限，各⼤机构均表⽰将持续跟踪，尚未将其纳
⼊估值框架。

## Document 301: France - 2027 Budget_ Fiscal consolidation must now resume (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France - 2027 Budget_ Fiscal consolidation must now resume (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

2027 Budget: Fiscal consolidation must now resume

The 2027 budget, adopted by the French Council of Ministers today, calls for a reduction in France’s fiscal 
deficit to 5% of GDP in 2027 (down from 5.4% in 2026). This is a first step toward s the multi-year fiscal 
consolidation needed to stabilise the public debt-to-GDP ratio. This ratio is expected to rise further in 2027 
and until the deficit is reduced to 3% of GDP. Furthermore, given the increase in some expenditures (+0.6 pp 
of GDP for debt service and defence spending), the projected fiscal effort amounts to 1 pp. Nearly one-third 
of this effort would come from higher compulsory levies, and two -thirds from curbing government and 
social spending.

2027 Draft finance law: A step in the right direction. The government is forecasting an improvement in the fiscal 
deficit from 5.4% of GDP in 2026 to 5% in 2027. This projection is based on a credible growth assumption of 1% 
(see our analysis of French growth drivers). To achieve this target, compulsory levies (CL) are expected to rise to 
44.2% of GDP, an increase of 0.3 pp (the same increase as in 2026, compared with +0.9 pp in 2025). The public 
spending-to-GDP ratio would decline by 0.2 pp to 56.9%. The effort required to reduce the deficit to 5% is greater 
than it appears. Indeed, some expenditures will increase (interest payments by 0.4 pp and defence spending by 
0.2 pp). Consequently, the fiscal effort for next year amounts not to 0.4 pp, but to 1 pp of GDP. One-third of this will 
be achieved through increased CL, and two-thirds through spending cuts. Social spending will contribute, notably 
through the cancellation of EUR 3 billion in social contribution reductions for low-wage earners, as well as efforts 
totaling EUR 6 billion each in the areas of pensions and healthcare. Social spending shows the largest deviation 
from pre-COVID levels, with an increase representing 1.2 pp of GDP, nearly half of the gap between the 2026 
budget balance and a 3% deficit. Government spending, excluding interest payments and defence, is expected to 
be frozen in nominal terms.

A compromise seems likely despite a tight schedule. The French National Assembly will start reviewing the 
budget on Tuesday 13 October. The government will need to ensure that a majority vote against the draft budget 
does not form. The current circumstances, including increasing sovereign bond yields and the upcoming 
presidential election, call for a consensus based on fiscal consolidation of the size proposed by the government. If 
Parliament does not pass a budget by the end of the year, the government will have to choose between 
implementing the government’s budget proposal by executive order ( only possible if there was no parliamentary 
vote) or through a special law (as in 2025 and 2026). A special law would enable tax collection , while the 
government and social security agencies could continue to issue debt. In this case, the 2026 budget would apply 
in 2027 (without indexation) until Parliament passes a budget in the first few weeks of the year. This must occur 
before the end of February (when Parliament’s session ends, earlier than usual due to the presidential election).

The one-year postponement of the 5% budget deficit target has a measurable impact. The gap between the 
budget adopted for 2026 (deficit of 5% of GDP) and the actual budget outcome (deficit of 5.4%) is due to 
unforeseeable circumstances (the conflict in Iran  and extreme drought). However, postponing the deficit target  
previously set for 2026 to 2027 comes at a cost: 1) the public debt-to-GDP ratio is expected to reach 121% in 2027 
according to our forecasts (1 pp above our scenario with a 5% target in 2026 and a deficit reduced to 4.5% in 2027), 
and 2) the (upcoming) postponement to 2032 of the 3% of GDP deficit target (a commitment that most presidential 
candidates plan to make) rather than in 2029 (the government had committed to this with the European Commission 
in 2024). In 2032, the public debt-to-GDP ratio would stabilise at 124% of GDP (instead of 121.5% if the deficit had 
been reduced to 5% of GDP as early as 2026 and to 3% of GDP by the end of the decade).

Another delay in fiscal consolidation would further erode France’s credibility. This new delay could stem 
either from a decision by the government formed in May 2027 to immediately implement part of its agenda, or from 
the enactment of a special law that would remain in effect until (just after) the likely legislative elections in June  
2027  or  for  the  entire  year.  If  the  government  emerging  from  the  elections  were  to  resume  fiscal  
consolidation only in 2028 (with a deficit of 5% of GDP that it projects to reduce to 3% by 2032), public debt would 
reach 126% of GDP by that date (a one-year delay in fiscal consolidation would result in an additional 2 pp of 
debt). This further delay could potentially undermine the credibility of the next government, given that budget 
targets were  not met in three out of four years between 2023 and 2026, a situation that would be costly amid a 
sharp rise in long-term interest rates. Furthermore, a higher deficit would increase the amount of debt to be issued 
within an already  tight schedule. The Agence France Trésor (AFT) has announced debt issuances totaling EUR 
340 billion in 2027 (an  increase  of  EUR  20  billion  compared  to  2026).  This  increase  is  due  to  the  maturing  
COVID  related  debt (beginning in 2027 and continuing largely through 2030), while the amount of debt to be 
issued to finance the deficit will remain stable compared to 2026. A decrease from this last driver would be needed 
in subsequent years so that  the AFT’s debt issuance can stabilise.

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of the Advanced Economies Team, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

持 1450 亿美元不变。

公司宣布创设新的⽆投票权 C 类普通股，计划于 2027 年 1 ⽉⽣效。这⼀安排旨在为公司的 AI 投资

⼀股将获得⼀股新股，这实质上等同于⼀次 “ ⼀拆⼆ ” 拆股。
Meta 发⾏⽆投票权新股，换取扎克伯格承诺留居加州
Meta 与扎克伯格达成协议，将向其发⾏⽆投票权 C 类股票。作为交换，扎克伯格承诺未来⼋年
继续居住在加州或 Meta 总部所在的其他司法辖区。

这项安排也与加州拟议中的亿万富豪财富税有关。扎克伯格曾表⽰，相关税收可能迫使其出售
具有投票权的 B 类股票以筹集税款，使其在公司的投票权⽐例降⾄ 53% 以下。
根据协议，扎克伯格可以⽴即出售所获 C 类股票的 20% ，相当于其整体经济权益的 10% ，其余股
份的出售限制将在⼋年内逐步解除。
M e t a 特 别 委 员 会 称 ， 确 保 扎 克 伯 格 在 公 司 总 部 办 公 ⾄ 关 重 要 ， 尤 其 是 在 公 司 与 M e t a
Superintelligence Labs 密切合作、 AI 竞争⽇益激烈之际。
Meta ⾸席财务官 Susan Li 则表⽰， C 类股票主要作为融资⼯具，帮助公司更灵活地为 AI 业务筹
资，同时避免稀释现有股东的投票权。
⼴告业务保持增⻓，第四季度营收指引超预期
⼴告业务仍是 Meta 业绩增⻓的主要⽀撑。第三季度，公司⼴告展⽰量同⽐增⻓ 10% ，平均⼴告
价格上涨 14% 。家庭⽇活跃⼈数达到 36.4 亿，同⽐增⻓ 3% 。

Meta 预计第四季度营收为 730 亿⾄ 760 亿美元，区间中值为 745 亿美元，较上年同期增⻓约
24% 。该指引⾼于 LSEG 汇总的分析师平均预期 730 亿美元左右，显⽰公司对年末⼴告需求和业
务增⻓保持信⼼。
⼴告业务的增⻓也为 AI 投资提供了重要⽀撑。 Meta 正将 AI 应⽤于⼴告投放和定向等环节，同时
尝试通过新的 AI 产品拓展⼴告以外的收⼊来源。不过，新业务的商业化进展能否匹配基础设施投

AI 助⼿ Muse 成商业化核⼼，但障碍犹存
Meta 将消费者 AI 助⼿ Muse 定位为整体战略的核⼼，并围绕负责该产品的 Meta 超级智能实验室对
公司架构进⾏重组，同时宣布推出⾯向企业客⼾销售 AI ⼯具的新业务线。
据市场情报机构 SensorTower 数据， Muse ⾃ 9 ⽉上线以来下载量已超过 1000 万次，该应⽤提供
每⽉ 20 美元和 100 美元两档订阅⽅案。

costs are keeping physical crude

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Low inventories and higher shipping costs will keep oil and
fuel prices elevated, with Brent futures forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing to US$77/bbl by Q4 2027
as supply recovers. Refined fuels and LNG prices should

ease more slowly. Risks are two-sided: escalation could
push Brent towards US$150/bbl, while an early US-Iran
agreement could see a faster normalisation in supply with
prices returning close to pre-conflict levels by end-2027.
This article was first published in the October Market Outlook .
Brent crude is back above US$100/bbl on fading confidence
in a lasting US-Iran agreement and intermittent attacks on
shipping. Buffers remain depleted, with the IEA estimating

global observed inventories were 507mb lower at the end of
August than at the start of the conflict.
This comes despite reported crude exports through the Strait of
Hormuz recovering to around 70% to 90% of pre-conflict levels
in late September. Saudi and UAE exports through the Yanbu and
Fujairah pipelines continue, with the East-West pipeline back to
normal after the early September attacks. Tanker crossings have
risen, although transponders remain switched off, leaving visible

transit at 1% to 4% of pre-conflict levels, while cargo transfers in
the Gulf of Oman have increased as an alternative route.
However, these workarounds are slower and more costly. Ship-
to-ship transfers can take 5 to 10 days, US escorts through
the Gulf via a route close to the Oman shoreline cause delays
while some tankers are taking longer routes. Fewer ships are
also available, with many redeployed to other markets. Higher
insurance premiums are adding to costs, with Gulf-to-China

tanker rates above US$0.9mn a day, around 15x higher than a
year ago. With prompt barrels (available for physical delivery)
scarce, the gap between physical and futures prices widened
again, with Dated Brent around US$136/bbl in early October
against US$101/bbl for front-month futures.
Oil and fuel prices push higher ...
Refined prices have risen more than crude
Refined product markets were already tight going into the
conflict. Reduced Gulf supply, exacerbated by Ukrainian strikes

on Russian refineries, has added to that tightness. Together
with higher shipping costs and rising Dated Brent prices,
Singapore Gasoil 10ppm (diesel) has roughly doubled since the
Strait closed, compared with around 40% for crude futures.
Consequently, Singapore gasoil crack spread widened
to an average of around US$36.1/bbl in early October,
from US$17.5/bbl across 2025. Alongside the Australian
government’s excise tax returning to normal, after it had been

halved, this has lifted national diesel and petrol prices to
around A$2.83/L and A$2.41/L.
Outlook
We do not see a lasting US-Iran agreement until at least
mid-2027. In the meantime, we assume there will not be a
return to full-scale military intervention. However, periodic
Iranian attacks on ships and facilities are expected to continue,
as will the US blockade of Iranian exports, keeping a supply
risk premium in place. We look for Brent futures to average

around US$101/bbl in Q4, then ease gradually as demand
from refiners and governments to rebuild stocks partly offsets
recovering supply. As the premium fades and the market
moves into surplus in late 2027, we forecast Brent at around
US$77/bbl in Q4 2027, easing towards US$70/bbl in 2028 as
Middle East production fully recovers.
Oil balance to gradually improve over 2027
Global oil supply is expected to rise by 4.0mb/d (4.0%) to
103.7mb/d in 2027, following a 6.1% fall in 2026. Most of

this reflects a gradual recovery in Gulf output, with Houthi
Sian Fenner, Head of Business & Industry Economics
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Luka Belobajdic, Economist

### Page 3

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
attacks on Red Sea shipping keeping Saudi production below
its pre-conflict level until H2 2027. Meanwhile, we look for UAE
production to continue to improve over 2027 and lift above
pre-conflict levels from 2028, as it is no longer constrained by

OPEC quotas. We do not expect a meaningful return of Iranian
oil until an agreement is reached, and damage sustained during
the conflict means production will take time to recover.
We expect global oil demand to be 103.7mb/d in 2026, 0.8mb/d
(0.7%) higher than in 2025, and to rise a further 1.9mb/d (1.8%)
to 105.6mb/d in 2027. Chinese crude imports will continue to
recover over the coming months as refiners restock ahead of
Lunar New Year, but we expect only a gradual return to pre-

conflict levels, as the electrification of transport limits the
recovery in Chinese demand. Globally, demand for diesel and jet
fuel recovers as transport and aviation activity picks up, although
some demand destruction and efficiency gains are expected
to be permanent. The conflict has also highlighted strategic
vulnerabilities, particularly across Asia, so we expect a push to
rebuild reserves adding around 0.5% to demand over 2027.
Fuel prices to ease more slowly than crude

Refined product supply is expected to recover more slowly
than crude, with Gulf refinery runs not returning to pre-war
levels until at least late 2027. Outside the Gulf, there has
been some constructive news. The US has lifted sanctions
on Russian diesel until April 2027, with Russia saying it will
supply more than 4Mt over the coming months, while China
has lifted its ban on refined product exports. However, we
are cautious about how much this will add to the market in

the short term. Much of the Russian supply is headed to the
US and will depend on Russia’s ability to restore refining and
export capacity, while doubts remain over the Russia-Ukraine
energy ceasefire announced by President Trump. China still
manages exports through quotas, which are likely to increase
only gradually as domestic stocks are rebuilt. Refinery runs
should lift ahead of Lunar New Year, but any increase in
exports will take time to reach regional markets.

With product supply recovering slowly and depleted inventories
being rebuilt, Singapore gasoil crack is likely to stay above its
long-run average even as Brent eases, narrowing from US$71/
bbl in Q4 to US$27/bbl by Q4 2027. With Singapore Gas 10ppm
prices, shipping and insurance costs to remain elevated and
easing only gradually in 2027, we forecast Australian diesel
to retail at an average of 274¢/L in Q4, easing to 200¢/L in Q4
2027, and petrol at 230¢/L and 187¢/L respectively.

LNG prices supported through 2027
Japanese LNG prices surged 15.2%mth in September,
averaging US$25.4/MMBtu as supply disruptions in Qatar
and its inability to divert exports around the Strait of Hormuz
tightened markets and intensified competition between
importers. While prices are now more than double pre-conflict
levels, demand destruction across Asia is providing some
offset to tight supply
Our Q4 forecast for Japanese LNG is now US$24.5/mmbtu and

we expect the market to remain structurally tight, with supply
recovery likely to be gradual and prices not returning to pre-
conflict levels until around 2028. Still, Australia’s domestic
gas market should remain insulated, supported by the FY2027
Federal Budget’s domestic gas reservation scheme, which
requires exporters to supply the domestic market with the
equivalent of up to 20% of gas exports. So far this year,
despite the increase in LNG spot prices in Asia, Australian

natural gas prices have remained under the $12 per GJ cap
that has proposed by the Commonwealth government. We are
expecting this to continue well into 2027.
Risks around our oil price outlook are two-sided
Talks are still ongoing in the background, with the US and
Iran exchanging proposals to reopen the Strait, but the gap
between them remains wide. The conflict has also expanded
to the Red Sea and, with inventories sharply drawn down,
there is a smaller buffer to absorb a further shock.

Using the Oxford Economics model, we assess an escalation
scenario, where energy markets face a deeper and more
persistent disruption as the conflict in the Middle East
intensifies. In this case, Brent futures rise to around US$150/
bbl in H1 2027, only dipping to below US$100/bbl in Q2 2028.
We also consider an early resolution scenario, where shipping
and Gulf supply normalise faster, returning Brent to near pre-
conflict levels by end-2027.
Escalation scenario

In the escalation scenario we assume:
Talks stall and the conflict escalates:  the gap between the
US and Iran proves too wide and the US resumes strikes after
the midterms. Iran responds with attacks on shipping and Gulf
energy infrastructure, damaging export, storage and refining
facilities. A lasting agreement is not reached until 2028.
Export routes are disrupted: more frequent attacks curtail
dark crossings of the Strait and ship-to-ship transfers in the

Gulf of Oman. Houthi attacks also cut off Saudi alternative
routes, forcing Gulf producers to cut output and Middle
East crude exports sharply lower. New routes and shipping
arrangements eventually support a recovery in Gulf flows, but
trade patterns take time to adjust.
... and risks remain and while they are two sided ...

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Product supply fails to recover:  The Russia–Ukraine energy
ceasefire does not hold and strikes on Russian refineries
continue, limiting how much diesel Russia can export,
even with the US sanctions waiver extended. China also

reintroduces its export ban to protect domestic supply.
Oil prices lift to around US$150/bbl in H1 2027: With little
buffer to absorb the supply loss, competition for physical
delivery intensifies, with physical prices well above futures
as prompt barrels remain scarce. Higher prices support
supply growth outside OPEC and drive demand destruction,
alongside further emergency stock releases, but a deeper
and more prolonged global deficit remains. Brent stays above

US$100/bbl until mid-2028 as governments rebuild reserves.
The Singapore gasoil crack widens and, with freight and
insurance costs also higher, Australian diesel and petrol prices
average around 247 cpl and 231 cpl at end 2027. Japanese
LNG also remains elevated around US$23.2/MMBtu in Q4
2027, $9.1 above our baseline.
Early resolution scenario
In the early resolution scenario we assume:
A durable agreement: The US and Iran agree terms to reopen
the Strait by the end of year, and, unlike the June interim deal,

the agreement holds, with progress towards a permanent
settlement in early 2027. Attacks on shipping cease and the
US blockade is lifted, allowing Iranian oil back into the market,
initially from storage. Vessels are quickly repositioned to the
Gulf, with freight and war-risk insurance easing from early
2027 rather than mid-year.
A faster recovery in supply:  With export routes secure, Gulf
production and damaged refinery and export capacity recover

sooner than in the baseline. Outside the Gulf, the US extends
its waiver on Russian diesel beyond April 2027 and China
raises export quotas as domestic stocks are rebuilt.
Demand settles below pre-conflict trend: Some demand
destruction and efficiency gains persist, particularly in China.
With inventories rebuilding faster, government restocking is
smaller and more gradual than in the baseline.
Prices fall quickly, but restocking provides a floor:  Brent

Brent futures ease to around US$88/bbl in Q1 2027 as the
premium for prompt physical barrels unwinds. As the market
moves into balance, Brent eases to around US$65/bbl by Q4
2027, close to pre-conflict levels.
Refined product prices ease faster on lower freight costs with
the Singapore gasoil crack narrowing. Australian diesel and
petrol prices fall to around 182 cpl and 170 cpl by Q4 2027. Gas
markets also ease, although damage to Qatar’s gas facility means

export capacity recovers gradually. Japanese LNG falls to around
US$11.9/MMBtu by end-2027, $2.1 below our baseline.
Economic impact
In the escalation scenario, higher fuel costs flow through to
transport, food and other energy-intensive goods such as
plastics and fertilisers. Australian headline CPI remains above
4.5% for much of 2027, with underlying inflation remaining
sticky at above 3% until late 2028. The RBA faces a difficult
trade off between much higher inflation and a significant

hit to activity, as households cut discretionary spending and
businesses defer investment. Higher LNG export earnings
provide only a moderate offset. In the early resolution scenario,
headline inflation is around 0.1ppt below the baseline in 2027.
New Zealand is more exposed in both scenarios, with food
and energy a larger share of its CPI and no offsetting energy
exports. Headline inflation is around 1ppt higher in the
escalation scenario in Q1 2027.

... the upside presents a more dramatic shift in prices

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
SEPTEMBER CPI PREVIEW
Setting the pace for the run into year end
19 October 2026
Key points
• September CPI to rise 0.62%mth 
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr 
from 4.0%yr. The seasonally adjusted 
CPI lifts 0.5%mth, a step down from 
the 0.7% increase in August.  
• September Trimmed Mean (TM) is 
set to lift 0.26%mth holding the 
annual pace steady at 3.6%yr. 
• As we noted in our review of the 
August CPI, our September quarter 
CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr 
while the TM remained 0.9%qtr.  
• Auto fuel surged ahead of our 
expectations so along with signs of 
increase pass-through we lifted our 
December quarter CPI to 1.3%qtr/3.9%yr 
and the TM estimate to 0.9%qtr/3.5%yr. 
• This is a 0.8ppt increase to the CPI 
estimate, and a 0.2ppt increase to the 
TM estimate, highlighting inflation 
momentum meaningfully above 
the RBA’s current expectations.

### Page 2

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts 
are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts 
are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Risks to September baseline
We view the risks around the September CPI outcome as broadly 
balanced. Waning spending momentum outside of fuel may make 
it difficult for businesses to pass through higher costs in the near 
term. However, some of these pricing pressures may be deferred 
rather than forgone. The October ban on card surcharging may 
prompt a greater-than-usual degree of price resetting, allowing 
businesses to recover some of the margin pressures that have built 
up in recent months.
Risks to housing are mixed. Trade materials suppliers continued 
to announce above-average price increases through September 
(around 8%). While soft conditions in the established housing 
market may limit the extent to which these costs are passed 
through to new dwelling prices, they nevertheless present an 
upside risk to housing-related inflation, particularly maintenance 
& repair and insurance costs. Growth in advertised rents also 
continues to ease but these new leases take some time to flow 
through to average rents paid measured in the CPI. 
A kick up in October ...
We have a solid preliminary estimate for the October CPI of 
0.6%mth lifting the annual pace to 4.8%yr. It is true that the key 
driver is an 8.9% increase in auto fuel but we estimate the TM to 
lift 0.4%mth/3.7%yr highlighting broader inflationary pressures. 
We expect that the removal of credit charge surcharges and 
resulting increase in retail prices, at most, will contribute 0.1ppt 
but only just so see it as more of a rounding error. Nevertheless, 
these changes come at a time of a significant upstream price 
pressure, particularly for SMEs, so there is some risk of broader 
price adjustments. As such, we expect marginally larger than 
usual price adjustments in October particularly in hospitality 
and household services. Being the first month of the quarter the 
October lift sets a higher base for the quarterly average estimates 
resulting in an upgrade to our December quarter estimate.
We have included a table with a monthly profile for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to June 2027. At 
June 2027 we have an annual pace of 3.7%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI and 3.3%yr for the TM. There is also a 
table breaking down the monthly components of the CPI through 
the December quarter. 
... highlights stronger inflationary pressure ...
Our September quarter CPI estimate is unrevised at 1.3%qtr 
with the annual pace steady at 3.9%yr. At the margin there 
was a 0.04ppt increase due to a lift in auto fuel but this is 
1.29%qtr at two decimal places so still rounds to 1.3%. 
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% in the September 
quarter highlighting that part of the increase is due to usual 
seasonality. Our TM estimate for Q3 has a small downward 
revision of 0.03ppt to 0.88% at two decimal place so our 
published estimate is also unrevised at 0.9%qtr. 
... resulting in an upward revision to Q4 2026
Westpac’s December quarter CPI estimate has been boosted 
from a 0.5%qtr to 1.3%qtr. Driving most of this change from 
our previous estimate is the 15.3% increase in autofuel, it

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
was 1.9%, contributing 0.55ppt to the 0.8ppt increase in the 
quarterly estimate. Outside of a 0.09ppt contribution to the 
revision from holiday travel & accommodation (revised up from 
4.2% to 5.6%) all else was via small contributions for a large 
number of expenditure categories. This is why there is a 0.2ppt 
increase in our September quarter TM estimate from 0.7%qtr 
to 0.9%qtr.
A disinflationary impulse kicks in for 2027
We now expect auto fuel prices to peak in October (240 cpl 
for petrol, 284 cpl for diesel) and then to trend lower through 
2027 (184 cpl for petrol and 194 cpl for diesel by December 
2027) leading to a 13.6% falling in auto fuel in the first half 
of 2027 subtracting 0.46ppt off the CPI over the same period 
of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the 
auto fuels profile please see: “ Persistent energy pressures 
have further to run ” 
The first half of 2027 will also see an almost 5% decline in 
holiday travel & accommodation which will subtract a further 
0.29ppt off the CPI. However, outside of that any broader 
moderation in inflation is expected to be more muted hence 
we have the TM printing 0.7%qtr for each quarter in 2027.  
Critical to this moderation in core inflation is our expect step 
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab -
lished home prices. From a 1.1%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we 
see dwelling price increasing by 0.7% in the Q4 2026, and 
then 0.6% in both Q1 and Q2 2027 before dropping to 0.5%qtr 
in Q3 and Q4 2027. This will see dwelling price inflation in the 
CPI drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 3.0%yr by June 2027 
then down to 2.2%yr by December 2027. 
At least for the near term a key uncertainty remains the extent 
to which businesses are able to pass through ongoing labour 
and non-labour cost pressures and how this unwinds through 
2027 as energy prices ease and the domestic economy slows 
under the weight of higher interest rates and falling estab -
lished home prices. There is a risk that firms face greater 
resistance to price increases if discretionary household spend -
ing softens by more than expected. That said, these headwinds 
may be at least partially offset by the stronger starting point 
for household incomes, which has proven more resilient than 
previously anticipated, and by higher energy prices due to the 
ongoing conflict in the Middle East.
That said, there is also the risk that the disinflationary pulse by 
the second half of 2027 could be greater than what we antici-
Quarterly forecast inflation profile

pate. Another rate hike in November, along with a deeper than 
expected fall in established home prices, could be the prover -
bial “straw” resulting in a larger than expected fall in demand 
and thus a more significant moderation in price setting behav -
iour. It is also possible that our auto fuel prices fall by more 
than we anticipate. For while we have spot crude prices to fall 
to about pre-crisis levels by end 2027, a combination of a slow 
recovery in refining capacity, constrained return of physical 
supplies and higher shipping cost keep Australian fuel prices 
above their pre-conflict level. Should all these factors improve 
fast than we expect, and auto fuel prices return to pre-conflict 
level if not lower, then headline inflation could dip below 2%yr 
and the TM would be closer to 2.5%yr (the mid point if the 
RBA’s inflation target), by end 2027.

達發 ESG 評等

電子製造業 ESG 評等分配表

資料來源:臺灣指數公司，國泰證期研究部整理

達發 6526 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
由網入光，發光發熱 
投資與評價 
1. 達發 3Q26 受惠光通放量與商務耳機出貨暢旺，營收創單季新高；
4Q26 光通持續成長，並同步推進 100G 量產與 OOB 新應用導入，營
運為近五年同期新高。 
2. 單通道 200G SerDes 與 25G PON 持續推進，50G/100G 光通、10G 
PON 與 OOB 新應用同步放量，高價值產品占比提升帶動產品組合優
化，預估 26 年 EPS 20.95 元；27 年 EPS 28.36 元，營運創歷史新高。 
3. 目前達發股價為 783 元，約 28 倍 FY27E EPS，位於近四年本益比參考
區間 20-50 倍中間偏下位置。考量光通進入高速成長期，OOB 帶動
AIDC 新應用，轉型方向明確，初次評等給予買進，以近四年本益比參
考區間平均值 35 倍作為評價基礎，目標價 995 元(35x FY27E PER)。

重點更新 
光通與商務耳機出貨暢旺，預期達發 3Q26 營運創近四年單季新高 
達發 3Q26 光通單通道 50G 受惠替換需求與新增標案持續放量 ， 乙太網受惠
低軌衛星規格升級，2.5G PHY 與交換器晶片出貨成長，藍牙則進入消費旺
季，商務耳機出貨增加，營收季增 10.4%至 69.41 億元，創單季新高。毛利
率受記憶體、NOR Flash 及封測成本上升影響，預估季減 2.1 個百分點至
49.1%，營業費用 23.35 億元 （+2.7% QoQ），營業利益 10.73 億元(+13.8% 
QoQ)；預估稅後淨利季增 11.1%至 9.83 億元，EPS 5.87 元，營運創近四年
單季新高。

光通逐季放量，達發 4Q26 營運為近五年同期新高 
達發 4Q26 進入季節性淡季，惟光通逐季放量，預期單通道 100G 逐步進入
量產，乙太網新一代 10G PHY 亦開始出貨，預估營收 64.40 億元（-7.2% 
QoQ） ，季減幅度小於往年。毛利率受上游成本持續上漲影響，惟受惠高毛利
光通持續放量， 預估毛利率持平為 49.1%，營業費用 22.05 億元（-5.6% 
QoQ），營業利益 9.57 億元（-10.8% QoQ）；預估稅後淨利 8.99 億元（-
8.56% QoQ），EPS 5.36 元，營運為近五年同期新高。

鍾沛寰 / IC 設計 
+886-2-2326-9899#1750/ parisphchung@cathayfut.com.tw

### Page 2

訪談內容 
光通與商務耳機出貨暢旺，預期達發 3Q26 營運創近四年單季新高 
達發 3Q26 藍牙進入消費旺季，商務耳機專案持續出貨，高階應用占比提升，惟上半年提前備貨效應逐步消退，藍
牙音訊出貨成長幅度相對溫和。光通方面，單通道 50G 受惠替換需求與新增標案持續放量，大型 CSP 替換專案亦
於下半年開始出貨，搭配出貨的 TIA 搭載率同步提升；乙太網受惠低軌衛星客戶由 1G 升級至 ASP 較高的 2.5G 
PHY，交換器晶片出貨持續成長；寬頻客戶下單節奏則回歸終端需求，3Q26 營收季增 10.4%至 69.41 億元，創單
季新高。 
毛利率方面，記憶體、NOR Flash 及封測成本上升，而產品漲價尚未反映，惟高毛利光通占比提升，部分抵銷成本
壓力，預估毛利率季減 2.1 個百分點至 49.1%。營業費用 23.35 億元（+2.7% QoQ） ，營收規模擴大帶動營業利益
10.73 億元（+13.8% QoQ） ；預估稅後淨利季增11.1%至 9.83 億元，EPS 5.87 元，營運創近四年單季新高。

圖一 : Airoha Revenue and Gross Margin Trend Forecast, 1Q25~4Q27 
資料來源:達發，國泰證期研究部整理

光通逐季放量，預期達發 4Q26 營運為近五年同期新高 
達發 4Q26 藍牙音訊進入季節性淡季，惟產品漲價預期於 1Q27 逐步反映，部分客戶提前於 4Q26 備貨，藍牙出貨
衰退幅度可望優於往年 。 光通方面 ， 單通道50G 延續替換需求 ，100G 預期於 4Q26 完成客戶驗證後逐步進入量產 ，
光通營收持續逐季成長；乙太網新一代 10G PHY 於 2H26 量產，並隨 BMC 出貨切入 AI 資料中心 OOB 應用，預
估 4Q26 營收 64.40 億元（-7.2% QoQ），較 4Q25 衰減幅度大幅縮小，淡季不淡。 
毛利率方面，封測價格持續調漲，產品漲價要到 1Q27 才逐步反映，惟高毛利光通占比持續提升，抵銷成本上漲壓
力，預估毛利率持平於49.1%。營業費用 22.05 億元 （-5.6% QoQ） ，營業利益9.57 億元 （-10.8% QoQ，+100.6% 
YoY） ，預估稅後淨利8.99 億元（-8.6% QoQ，+68.8% YoY），EPS 5.36 元，營運為近五年同期新高。

### Page 3

光通營收大幅成長，預期達發 26 年營運創近五年新高 
達發 26 年光通營收受惠可插拔式光收發模組需求增加、客戶規模擴大及替換需求，預期成長至25 年的四倍以上，
占整體營收比重達 mid-single digit， 其中單通道50G 為主要貢獻 ， 搭配出貨的TIA 搭載率亦持續提升 。 寬頻方面 ，
歐美運營商規格升級帶動 10G PON 預估占寬頻營收比重接近三成，10G PON ASP 約為 G-PON 的兩倍，推升產
品組合優化，並切入 AI 資料中心 OOB 架構；乙太網受惠低軌衛星客戶由 1G 升級至 ASP 較高的 2.5G PHY，加上
交換器市占提升，為成長僅次於光通的產品線；藍牙音訊受惠商務耳機專案落地，高階應用占比提升，預估 26 年
營收年增 20.1%至 251.26 億元，創歷史新高。 
毛利率方面，記憶體、NOR Flash 及封測成本上漲壓抑下半年毛利率，惟光通、10G PON 及商務耳機等高價值產
品占比提升，部分抵銷成本壓力，預估 26 年毛利率年減 1.5 個百分點至 50.4%。營業費用 88.75 億元（+11.5% 
YoY） ，營收規模擴大帶動費用率由25 年的 38.0%降至 35.3%，營業利益 37.90 億元（+30.3% YoY） ；預估稅後淨
利年增 24.4%至 35.16 億元，EPS 20.95 元，營運創近五年新高。

圖二 : Airoha Revenue and Gross Margin Trend Forecast, 2022~2027

資料來源:達發，國泰證期研究部整理

預期 100G 擴大放量，達發 27 年營運為歷史新高 
達發 27 年光通動能由單通道 100G 接棒，100G 已於多家模組廠開案，800G 方案達到指標客戶低功耗要求，預期
27 年逐季放量，100G DSP ASP預估約為 50G 的兩倍；單通道50G 受惠替換需求維持穩健成長，100G 搭配的 TIA
搭載率預期高於 50G，並開始單獨出貨搭配其他供應商的 DSP。預期 27 年光通營收較 26 年再成長三倍以上，占
營收比重提升至一成五左右 。 寬頻方面 ，25G PON 預期於 27 年量產 ，OOB 架構市場規模於 26 至 28 年逐年翻倍 ，
預估資料中心 PON 設備市場 26 至 30 年 CAGR 達 52%；乙太網新一代 10G PHY 貢獻提升，2.5G 交換器亦將放
量，預估 27 年營收年增 17.9%至 296.21 億元，創歷史新高。 
毛利率方面，產品價格結構調整逐步反映成本變動，加上高毛利光通占比提升，預估 27 年毛利率年增 0.6 個百分
點至 51.0%。研發費用隨 100G、200G SerDes 及 25G PON 等新產品持續投入，惟 200G SerDes IP 授權金認列
為無形資產並分期攤銷，對費用影響有限，預估營業費用 98.51 億元（+11.0% YoY） ，費用率降至33.3%，營業利
益 52.49 億元（+38.5% YoY） ；預估稅後淨利年增35.3%至 47.60 億元，EPS 28.36 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

光通與 OOB 切入 AI 資料中心，達發成長動能升溫 
達發 3Q26 受惠光通放量與商務耳機出貨暢旺，預期營運創近四年單季新高；4Q26 光通持續成長，營運為近五年
同期新高。26 年光通營收預期成長至 25 年的四倍以上，10G PON 占寬頻營收比重接近三成，並與乙太網 PHY 同
步切入 AI 資料中心 OOB 架構；27 年單通道 100G 接棒放量，25G PON 與新一代 10G PHY 開始貢獻，產品應用
由消費性電子與家用寬頻逐步延伸至 AI 資料中心光通訊及帶外管理，預估 26 年及 27 年 EPS 分別為 20.95 元及
28.36 元，成長動能明確，初次評等給予買進投資建議。 
目前達發股價為 783 元(28X FY27E EPS)，位於近四年本益比參考區間 20-50 倍中間偏下位置。考量光通進入高速
成長期，預期 27 年營收占比提升至一成五，100G DSP ASP 約為 50G 的兩倍，高毛利產品占比提高帶動 27 年營
業利益年增 38.5%；加上 OOB 架構市場規模預期於 26 至 28 年逐年翻倍，資料中心 PON 設備市場 26 至 30 年
CAGR 預估達 52%，寬頻與乙太網同步受惠 AI 資料中心建置，產品組合轉型方向明確，以近四年本益比參考區間
平均值 35 倍作為評價基礎，目標價 995 元(35x FY27E EPS)。

產業概況 
AI 資料中心頻寬倍增，PAM4 DSP 隨單通道速率升級 
可插拔式光收發模組為 AI 資料中心 Scale-out 網路中伺服器與交換器間資料傳輸的主要介面 ， 模組內由PAM4 DSP
負責訊號等化與時脈回復，TIA 將光檢測器輸出的電流訊號轉換為電壓訊號，Driver 則負責驅動雷射。PAM4 調變
每個符號承載 2 位元，使單通道傳輸速率由 50G 逐步升級至 100G 及 200G，對應光模組容量由 200G、400G 提
升至 800G 與 1.6T。隨 GPU 叢集規模擴大與交換器頻寬倍增，2025 年 800G PAM4晶片組銷售額成長逾一倍，預
估 1.6T 晶片組 2026 年銷售額將超過 20 億美元。 
不同於過往世代全面切換，800G 與 1.6T 預期將並行部署，加上既有 AI 資料中心光模組逐步進入替換期，單通道
50G 與 100G 需求仍具支撐。晶片價值隨速率提升，單通道 100G DSP ASP 預估約為 50G 的兩倍；惟 2027 年後
線性驅動(LPO)與共封裝光學(CPO)滲透率提升，PAM4 DSP成長斜率預期趨緩，而近封裝光學(NPO)若光引擎不含
DSP，訊號補償將更仰賴 TIA 與 Driver，提升高速類比晶片的重要性。

圖三 : 光收發模組與主動式線材 IC 晶片組銷售預估，2025~2031F 
資料來源:LightCounting，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 5

PON 世代升級加速，OOB 開啟資料中心新應用 
被動光纖網路(PON)以光纖一對多傳輸，由局端 OLT 經無源分光器連接多個終端 ONU，為光纖到府的主流技術。
規格由 G-PON(下行 2.5Gbps)升級至 10G PON，並朝 25G PON 發展，網通世代轉換週期約五至七年；10G PON 
ASP 預估約為 G-PON 的兩倍，隨歐美運營商規格升級與東南亞滲透率提升，帶動 PON 晶片價值增加。 
AI 資料中心除負責運算的主網路外，另需獨立的帶外管理(OOB)網路監控伺服器狀態，以確保運算不中斷。傳統架
構需於每個機櫃頂端安裝管理型以太網交換器，並以銅線一對一連接伺服器，占用 1 至數個機櫃空間單位(RU)；導
入 PON 後，以免供電的無源分光器取代交換器，依實測數據可節省高達 99%的管理機櫃空間，並精簡布線與降低
功耗，讓機櫃容納更多運算節點。超大型雲端業者已陸續導入，預估資料中心PON 設備市場 26 至 30 年 CAGR 達
52%，累計支出逾 30 億美元；伺服器內 BMC 亦需以太網 PHY 傳輸管理訊號，帶動 PON 與 PHY 同步受惠。

圖四 : 超大型雲端業者資料中心 PON-based OOB 架構 
資料來源:Meta，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 6

公司簡介 
達發科技股份有限公司(Airoha Technology Corp., 6526 TT)成立於 2001 年，總部位於新竹縣竹北市，為聯發科集
團旗下 IC 設計公司。2021 年整併誠致科技、雷凌科技、絡達科技及聯發科IoT 部門並更名為達發科技，2022 年 6
月登錄興櫃，2023 年 10 月於證交所上市。公司業務分為有線網通基礎建設與無線邊緣 AI 兩大事業群：有線網通
涵蓋固網寬頻(G-PON、10G PON)、乙太網(PHY 與交換器晶片)及光通訊(PAM4 DSP 與 TIA)，應用由家用寬頻、
低軌衛星地面設備延伸至 AI 資料中心光通訊與帶外管理，並於 2024 年 7 月完成參與九暘私募，九暘為合併子公
司，布局 PoE 晶片；無線邊緣 AI 以藍牙音訊為主，聚焦商務、電競及聲學等中高階耳機市場，並延伸至藍牙傳輸
(電競鍵鼠、電子貨架標籤)及衛星定位(GNSS)晶片。預估 26 年有線網通與無線邊緣 AI 營收占比約各半，其中藍牙
約 40%、固網寬頻約 30%、乙太網約 14%、衛星定位約 10%、光通約 6%。

圖六 : 2026E 產品營收占比預估

資料來源:國泰證期研究部預估

### Page 7

ESG 政策 
達發以社會、經濟、環境及資訊揭露四大構面推動永續經營，已在員工照護、職業安全、董事會獨立性、資
訊安全及溫室氣體管理等面向建立相關制度，評估期間未發生重大人權、消費者權益、職業災害、誠信經營
及環境法規違規情事。

社會面：達發依循國際人權宣言制定人權政策並揭露執行結果，並將人權要求納入供應商評鑑與稽核。員工
管理方面，提供優於法規的休假、保險及身心健康措施，並以員工認股權與持股信託強化人才留任；職業安
全衛生管理系統已取得外部認證，並揭露工傷統計及減災目標。另建立績效評估及能力發展計畫，設定員工
多元化目標、揭露女性主管占比，並足額進用身心障礙員工。未來可完善人權盡職調查流程，並揭露員工滿
意度、訓練成效、性別薪酬比及客戶滿意度。

經濟面：達發每年召開 2 次以上法人說明會，董事長親自出席股東常會，並揭露議案表決結果。獨立董事席
次達半數以上，設置永續發展相關委員會並由董事長擔任最高負責人，且定期執行董事會外部績效評估。另
制定反貪腐、反競爭、內線交易防範及檢舉制度，風險管理政策經董事會通過，並設置資安專責單位、定期
測試資安機制。後續可強化接班規劃、設置提名委員會、提高任一性別董事席次至三分之一以上，並將永續
績效納入高階主管與董事薪酬。

環境面：氣候管理由董事會及管理階層監督，已揭露範疇一、二及範疇三排放，並設定量化中長期減碳、節
能及再生能源目標。達發亦制定水資源及廢棄物管理政策，揭露用水量及有害廢棄物產出量，並將環境表現
納入供應商遴選及稽核。惟尚未取得 ISO 14001 及碳盤查認證，未來可補充 1.5°C 以下及 3°C 以上氣候情境
分析、再生能源使用量及用水、廢棄物量化減量目標。

揭露面：達發依循 GRI 準則編製永續報告書，揭露重大性分析及 SDGs 對應作為，並提供英文版報告書及英
文網站。後續可導入 SASB 對應揭露及第三方確信，建立利害關係人回饋機制，並採用雙重重大性原則強化
ESG 資訊完整性。

1
AI代理將推升科技獲利
分析師／張乘維、謝孟璇、張偉漢
研究範疇／科技
科技產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/29
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《AI代理將持續推動科技與半導體獲利成長》
根據機構LSEG統計，受到AI代理推動GPU、記憶體、
客製化AI晶片，以及相關硬體獲利持續高成長 。機
構上調科技產業 4Q26、1Q27獲利年增率到 53.7%、
51.6%，本行樂觀看待科技產業發展後勢。
受到AI代理需求大增與大量低價的開放模型加入 ，
帶動2026年Token使用量呈現指數成長 。根據大型
語言模型 API聚合平台OpenRouter統計，週Token
使 用 量 由2026 年 2-3 兆 Tokens暴增至 9 月 129 兆

Tokens，短短9個月成長超過 40倍，顯示AI代理帶
動AI推論指數級擴張 。另外，Meta於9月發表個人
AI代理Muse應用軟體，可替用戶「管理郵件、訂餐
廳、填寫表格 、比價網購 、安排日程」，用戶關閉
軟體後會繼續完成任務 。預計也將有眾多企業在
Muse平台使用自己的 AI代理。機構統計Muse在開
放前12天美加 iOS下載量達 180萬，高於ChatGPT
同期的130萬，這代表AI推論需求仍將供不應求。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望
科技 正向 AI代理帶動算力需求大增，半導體2027年將續增三成。軟體新增AI代理營收，資安前景持續看好。
• AI代理將帶動2027半導體成長
三成 Pg2
• 客製化AI晶片2027年成長高於 4
成 Pg3
• AI代理對軟體公司營收提供新動
能，資安成為AI 落地的先決條件

2
吸納量
重點摘要 《AI代理與推論將帶動2027年半導體成長三成》
受到AI持續推動對資料中心 、高效運算 GPU/CPU
與HBM、DRAM、NAND等記憶體強勁需求 。機
構WSTS統計2026年上半導體市場規模達到 7,020
億美元、成長102%。其中記憶體成長高達302%、
邏輯IC成長45%。所以WSTS於8月上調全球半導體
2026年銷售額到 1.66兆美元 、年增108%(機構6月
時預估 1.51兆美元 、年增90%)，也同步上調 2027
年銷售額至 2.14兆美元、年增29%(機構6月時預估
1.91兆美元、年增27%)。記憶體和邏輯IC合計將佔
83%的半導體市場。機構預估2027年半導體銷售額
仍將由AI資料中心需要的相關晶片帶動 ，包含記憶

體將年增36%、邏輯IC將年增21%。
《博通上修AI營收預估，雲服務商與AI模型商持續
加大投資客製化AI晶片》
博通FY26Q3( 2026/5-2026/7 )營收、EPS優於預
期，公司將2027財年包括客製化AI晶片( AI ASIC )
及AI網路的AI營收由1,000億美元上修至1,150億美
元，並預估2028財年AI營收將再翻倍至 2,300億美
元 ， 超越市場原先約 1,800 億 美 元 的 預 估 。
Anthropic和Google的TPU部署正按計畫推進 ，目
前博通已向Anthropic和Google大量交付了TPU v7
Ironwood，同時開始為 Google生產交付 TPU v8i，
預計本季將大幅提升交付 TPU v8i的規模。另外，
OpenAI和Meta的AI晶片專案也有所進展 ，公司已
推出OpenAI第一代客製化AI晶片Jalapeño。
• 機構上調2027年半導體市場
到年增29%
• 博通上修2027財年AI營收預
測，並預估2028財年AI營收

將翻倍至2,300億美元。
AI代理與推論持續帶動記憶體、邏輯IC大幅成長
資料來源：WSTS，2026/8/6，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/29

吸納量
重點摘要 上述及後續專案將共同為博通 2027、2028財年AI
營收目標提供支撐 ，博通對未來AI營收的預估也顯
示雲端業者及 AI模型商正持續增加客製化 AI晶片投
資金額 。在客戶的 AI晶片部署進度上 ，博通預計
Anthropic在2026年將部署1吉瓦Ironwood系統、
2027年部署5吉瓦TPU v8i、2028年部署10吉瓦AI
晶片系統 ，預計Anthropic在2027年開始將成為博
通 最 大 客 製 化AI 晶 片 客 戶 ； 此 外， 公 司 也 預 計
OpenAI在2027年將部署 1.3吉瓦 Jalapeño、2028
年部署超過 5吉瓦Jalapeño及後續客製化 AI晶片。
由於市場預估客戶每部署 1吉瓦AI晶片系統約可為

博通帶來150~250億美元營收，根據博通公布的客
戶部署量，未來AI營收仍有上修機會。
《邁威爾上調 2027及2028財年營收預測 ，因市場
對資料中心互連、網路擴展產品需求強勁》
由於市場對資料中心互連 、擴展、客製化產品需求
強勁，邁威爾也上調了2027財年( 2026/2-2027/1 )
及2028財年( 2027/2-2028/1 )營收預測，將2027
財年營收預測由原先 115億美元上調至 120億美元
( YoY+45% )、2028財年營收預測由原先165億美
元上調至180億美元( YoY+50% ) 。邁威爾2028財
年成長潛力來自網路業務規模擴大 ，包括1.6T光模
組DSP、交換器，以及光學互連等新興需求；微軟
Maia 300、與Google的合作以及 XPU附加產品也
推升了2028財年客製化業務營收，市場對邁威爾AI
網通及客製化晶片項目的投資持續增加。
• 市場預估客戶每部署 1吉瓦
AI晶片系統約可為博通帶來
150~250億美元營收 ，未來
博通AI營收仍有上修機會。

重點摘要
零售客流量表
4
• AI 正從概念驗證轉向落地，並
產生可衡量的商業成果
《AI 正從概念驗證轉向落地，並產生可衡量的商業
成果》
9 越多家軟體龍頭不約而同揭露代理式 AI (Agentic
AI) 已由概念驗證階段跨入實質營收貢獻。益華電腦
(Cadence) 指出，其新推出的代理層採「消費加訂
閱」全新計費模式，且歷史經驗顯示效率提升不會
壓縮底層工具用量，一如 2006 年推出速度快 10 倍
的模擬器後客戶用量不減反增，原因在於晶片複雜
度呈指數成長，依台積電技術藍圖，未來 5 年晶片
複雜度將暴增48倍，人力根本無法同比例擴充。賽
富時 (Salesforce) 則與 Anthropic 共同推出
Claudeforce，並揭露頂級整合方案定價 550 美元，

較傳統業務團隊分散採購多套軟體之 1,300 美元支
出下降逾五成。近期財報數據顯示賽富時客戶加速
採用 AI 代理，AI 代理工作量加速成長並驅動相關
營收成長。MongoDB 的 Voyage 嵌入模型透過
Claude Code、Codex 等程式碼代理主動推薦，成
為全新的免費導流入口，單季即新增 2,900家 淨新
客戶；同時 MongoDB 也開始扮演前沿實驗室推理
記憶層角色，年化營收已達 5 億美元的語音 AI 獨角
獸 ElevenLabs 即因原生雲端資料庫無法承載代理
規模而轉單。Snowflake 則觀察到企業導入代理式
編碼工具後，資料遷移時程由過去以年為單位大幅
壓縮至一季內完成，帶動其由時間人力計價轉向成
果制計價的商業模式轉型。

### Page 5

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要
零售客流量表
5
• 資安已從單純的成本中心轉為
代理式AI落地的先決條件，預
算來源正從單一資安部門擴散
至整體 AI 治理預算。
《資安躍居代理式 AI 時代最確定的新稅基》
代理式 AI 普及的另一面，是攻擊面與治理風險同步
暴增，資安支出因此出現明確的預算擴張訊號。
CrowdStrike 執行長明確表示，客戶端此刻是「資
安部門首度替IT踩油門而非踩煞車」，公司並預估，
AI 基礎建設支出將由 2.7 兆美元擴大至 2030 年的
6 兆美元，AI 偵測與應變 (AIDR) 滲透率終將如當年
的防毒軟體一樣趨近 100%。Palo Alto Networks
則揭露，其利用多模型弱點掃描，六成弱點由自研
模型找出、三成靠 OpenAI、一成來自其他模型，

印證單一模型無法涵蓋所有攻擊面，企業勢必得靠
平台化廠商整合多重情報來源。執行長並直言，一
般企業平均仍在使用 30 至 40 套資安工具，資料無
法互通，正是 AI 得以強化既有龍頭護城河而非顛覆
產業的關鍵原因。ServiceNow 與 Google 則分別
從身分治理與弱點修復角度切入：前者將 7 億台已
納管設備擴大至涵蓋逾 40 億台新設備，導入「零
常設權限」概念因應代理身分的短暫性；後者透過
Wiz 加 CodeMender 形成掃描、修復、驗證的完
整閉環，帶動逾九成財星百大企業採用其資安服務。
資安已從單純的成本中心轉為代理式 AI 落地的先決
條件，預算來源正從單一資安部門擴散至整體 AI 治
理預算，具備海量原生遙測資料的平台型業者，將
持續享有資料規模帶來的護城河。
大摩基準情境預估網安市值 2028 達 3 千億美元，CAGR 23%
資料來源：摩根史坦利，2026/9/20，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/09/22
(市值，十億美元)

## Document 505: 中国経済見通し：不動産不況の底打ちに期待

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国経済見通し：不動産不況の底打ちに期待.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

中国

2026年 10月 22日 全9 頁

中国経済見通し：不動産不況の底打ちに期待 
見通しを2026年4.5％→4.6％、2027年4.2％→4.4％に引き上げ

調査本部 主席研究員 齋藤 尚登

[要約] 
 中国国家統計局によると、2026年7月～9月の実質GDP成長率は前年同期比4.5％（以
下、断りのない限り変化率は前年比、前年同期比）となった。1月～9 月は 4.6％と、
2026 年の政府成長率目標である 4.5％～5.0％のレンジ内となったが、 4 月～6 月
（4.3％）はこれを下回り、7月～9月は下限と同じだった。1月～9月の4.6％成長に対
する需要項目別寄与度は、最終消費支出が2.1％pt、総資本形成は1.3％pt、純輸出は
1.2％ptであった。1月～6月との比較では、内需が一段と減速した一方で、7月～9月
の貿易黒字の急増を背景に、純輸出の寄与度が上昇した。 
 当面の注目点は、2026年8月28日付で発表された住宅の予約販売から完成後販売への
移行と、10 月 1 日に開始された新規住宅ローンに対する財政による1％pt の利子補給
で、住宅需要がどれほど刺激されるかである。大和総研では、少なくとも当面は、不
動産不況による景気下振れリスクが限定的になるとみている。このため、中国経済見
通しを若干引き上げた。2026 年は従来の 4.5％から 4.6％に、2027 年は 4.2％から
4.4％に上方修正した。

2026年 7 月～9 月の実質 GDP 成長率は政府目標の下限と同じ 4.5％ 
中国国家統計局によると、2026 年 7 月～9 月の実質 GDP 成長率は前年同期比 4.5％（以下、
断りのない限り変化率は前年比、前年同期比）となった。1月～9 月は4.6％と、2026 年の政府
成長率目標である 4.5％～5.0％のレンジ内となったが、4 月～6 月（4.3％）はこれを下回り、
7 月～9 月は下限と同じだった。 
2026 年 1 月～9 月の 4.6％成長に対する需要項目別寄与度は、最終消費支出が 2.1％pt（1 月
～6 月は4.7％成長に対して2.2％pt）、総資本形成は1.3％pt（同1.7％pt）、純輸出は1.2％pt
（同 0.8％pt）であった。1 月～6 月との比較では、内需が一段と減速した一方で、後述する 7
月～9 月の貿易黒字の急増を背景に、純輸出の寄与度が上昇した。
